Resident Evil Revelations 2 (2015) — Complete PC Route

Spoiler-free total completion: both campaign routes, every record and reward, all Raid missions and medallions, Deluxe content, and verified Switch exclusives · 32 areas · 1676 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead — it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

Route and Edition Setup

You are here when: Steam's DLC manager, the title menu, and the Raid Mode vestibule.

The Windows Complete Season is primary; Deluxe add-ons and the verified Switch-only branch are audited separately.

Use the Complete Windows Release

Setup

HintBegin with the episodic campaign and Raid content installed together.
WhereInstall the Windows game, all four main episodes, both extra episodes, and every owned update.
Full detailOpen Steam's DLC manager, enable the campaign episodes and both extra episodes, finish the update, then launch Campaign and Raid Mode once so their local data is created.

Enable the Deluxe Content Set

Setup

HintThe most complete PC route adds maps, characters, costumes, and storage.
WhereEnable the Throwback pack, both Raid characters, four costumes, and all nine storage expansions.
Full detailIn Steam's DLC manager, tick every Deluxe component, relaunch, then confirm Gauntlet IX, two extra character slots, four costume entries, and expanded weapon, part, and album capacities can register.

Create a Clean Backup

Setup

HintSeveral choices alter later routes even though scenarios remain replayable.
WhereBack up the new local profile before Episode 1 and again before the Episode 3 choice.
Full detailClose the game before copying its local save through your normal backup method. Restore-test the copy on a spare location, then keep dated backups before Episode 3's final input and Episode 4's route lever.

Use Text-Only Locations

Guide Rule

HintNo owned gameplay footage exists for this title yet.
WhereUse the physical text cues; this pack intentionally contains no sketches or screenshots.
Full detailDo not generate substitute art or import another guide's images. Add visuals later only when they can be harvested from Sami's own recording with recorded provenance.

Episode 1 Side A Medal #01 (missable)

Scenario Medal

HintA scenario-specific action is tracked separately from ordinary completion.
WhereDuring Episode 1 Side A, satisfy this condition: five light melee knockdowns.
Full detailFocus the support partner's flashlight until an enemy reels, switch to the armed partner, and perform the prompted physical strike. Repeat on separate valid knockdowns until the result counter reaches five.

Scenario Medal

HintA scenario-specific action is tracked separately from ordinary completion.

Full detailAfter the normal episode clear, open Rewards, spend 1,000 BP on its Invisible entry, launch the mode, finish both scenario sides under its fixed rules, and verify the episode record unlocks.

Campaign Under Three Hours

Speed Clear

HintA direct route across all main episodes opens the final firearm reward.
WhereFinish all four main episodes with a combined clear time below three hours.
Full detailUse a separate practiced run, skip optional searches already completed, pause only outside recorded play time, finish both sides of every episode, and confirm the cumulative clear time is under 3:00:00.

Campaign Rewards and Galleries

You are here when: The Rewards, Special Settings, file viewer, concept gallery, and figurine viewer menus.

Neutral numbering protects identities and document subjects until the player chooses to open them.

Extra Episodes

You are here when: The two separately listed bonus episodes beneath the main campaign menu.

Both add-on episodes support Casual and Survival; their special records remain outside the main record-set total.

The Struggle Casual

Extra Episode Record

HintHunt efficiently during supply stages so the survival stages remain recoverable.
WhereComplete The Struggle Casual by meeting clear Casual route.
Full detailStart The Struggle on Casual, collect food before each gate, preserve ammunition for wave stages, and satisfy clear Casual route. Let the final result save before replaying.

The Struggle Survival

Extra Episode Record

HintHunt efficiently during supply stages so the survival stages remain recoverable.
WhereComplete The Struggle Survival by meeting clear Survival route.
Full detailStart The Struggle on Survival, collect food before each gate, preserve ammunition for wave stages, and satisfy clear Survival route. Let the final result save before replaying.

Extra Episode Record

HintHunt efficiently during supply stages so the survival stages remain recoverable.
WhereComplete The Struggle S Rank by meeting Survival S rank.
Full detailStart The Struggle on Survival, collect food before each gate, preserve ammunition for wave stages, and satisfy Survival S rank. Let the final result save before replaying.

The Struggle Full Rations

Extra Episode Record

HintHunt efficiently during supply stages so the survival stages remain recoverable.
WhereComplete The Struggle Full Rations by meeting fill complete ration gauge.
Full detailDuring the first scavenging stage, hunt animals until the ration meter is full; if needed, deliberately retry while retaining gathered food minus the spent continue. Reach the gate with the gauge completely filled.

Little Miss Unseen

Extra Episode Record

HintStudy patrols with the unseen partner before moving the vulnerable character.
WhereComplete Little Miss Unseen by meeting never get detected.
Full detailComplete every Little Miss stage without allowing an enemy detection state. Scout routes first, remain crouched behind cover, use smoke only at forced crossings, and restart any stage as soon as detection occurs.

Little Miss No Pointing

Extra Episode Record

HintStudy patrols with the unseen partner before moving the vulnerable character.
WhereComplete Little Miss No Pointing by meeting never use partner pointing.
Full detailComplete every Little Miss stage without using the partner's pointing command. You may switch viewpoints to study patrols, but never mark an enemy; restart the stage immediately if a marker is placed.

Raid Weapon Catalog

You are here when: Mission chests, unidentified albums, the jukebox, store, and weapon storage.

The AK-7 has no verified named rare counterpart; the other base weapons list their named variants separately.

MPM

Raid Weapon

HintEvaluate weapon albums from mission chests and watch the store rotation.
WhereObtain the base MPM at any level or tag.
Full detailOpen every chest during Raid clears, evaluate weapon albums at the jukebox, and inspect the store between missions until MPM appears. Keep one copy long enough for the weapon-catalog record to update.

Liberator

Rare Raid Weapon

HintHigher Raid difficulties improve the chance of a named weapon album.
WhereEvaluate albums until the named Liberator variant appears.
Full detailFarm late Very Hard or Code Red missions, open every available chest, evaluate the weapon albums, and retain the first Liberator copy. Its drop is random, so no particular mission can guarantee it.

Anti-Recoil

Raid Part

HintEvaluate part albums and preserve duplicates for the toolbox.
WhereObtain Anti-Recoil, then combine copies toward level 5.
Full detailOpen Raid chests, evaluate part albums until Anti-Recoil appears, and keep matching copies. Unlock the required toolbox tier, combine equal levels repeatedly, and verify the stored Anti-Recoil reaches level 5.

The one-thousand-clear record is included because true completion extends beyond mission variety alone.

Raid Gauntlet I

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet I, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Gauntlet I Normal Mission 1 — Clear

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet I Normal Mission 1 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Gauntlet I Normal Mission 1 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet I Normal Mission 1 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet I Normal Mission 1 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet I, Normal, Mission 1. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

WhereClear Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet I Very Hard Mission 6 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet I Very Hard Mission 6 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet I, Very Hard, Mission 6. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet I, Code Red, Mission 1 at or below its displayed recommended level.

Raid Gauntlet II

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet II, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet II, Normal, Mission 1 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet II, Normal, Mission 1, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet II, Hard, Mission 3.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet II, Hard, Mission 3. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet II, Hard, Mission 4 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet II, Hard, Mission 4 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Raid Gauntlet III

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet III, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Gauntlet III Normal Mission 1 — Clear

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet III Normal Mission 1 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Gauntlet III Normal Mission 1 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet III Normal Mission 1 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet III Normal Mission 1 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet III, Normal, Mission 1. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Raid Gauntlet IV

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet IV, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet IV Hard Mission 2 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Gauntlet IV Hard Mission 2 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet IV Hard Mission 2 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet IV Hard Mission 2 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet IV, Hard, Mission 2. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

WhereClear Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet IV Code Red Mission 5 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet IV Code Red Mission 5 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 5. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Gauntlet IV Code Red Mission 6 — Clear

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 6 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 6, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet IV Code Red Mission 6 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 6 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet IV, Code Red, Mission 6, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Raid Gauntlet V

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet V, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Gauntlet V Normal Mission 1 — Clear

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet V Normal Mission 1 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Gauntlet V Normal Mission 1 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet V Normal Mission 1 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet V Normal Mission 1 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet V, Normal, Mission 1. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Raid Gauntlet VI

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet VI, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6 at or below its displayed recommended level.

Gauntlet VI Normal Mission 6 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet VI Normal Mission 6 — Genocide

Raid Medallion

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet VI Normal Mission 6 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet VI, Normal, Mission 6. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Raid Gauntlet VII

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet VII, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Raid Gauntlet VIII

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet VIII, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.

Raid Medallion

WhereClear Gauntlet VIII, Normal, Mission 4 without consuming any herb.

HintSearch every branch before ending the mission.
WhereDefeat every enemy spawned in Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 1 before using the goal.
Full detailSweep every room and optional branch in Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 1, wait for delayed waves, and check the remaining-enemy display where available. Use the goal only after every spawned target is defeated.

Gauntlet VIII Code Red Mission 1 — Completion

Raid Medallion

HintThe final medallion demands every other condition during one clear.
WhereEarn all four other medallions together in one run of Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 1.
Full detailSet your level at or below the recommendation, consume no herbs, defeat every spawned enemy, and reach the goal in the same uninterrupted run of Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 1. Confirm all five medallions illuminate together.

Gauntlet VIII Code Red Mission 2 — Clear

Raid Medallion

HintReach the goal once under any valid result.
WhereFinish Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2 and touch its goal marker.
Full detailSelect Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2, defeat or bypass enemies as the objective permits, complete any required switch or defense task, then use the goal marker and confirm the Clear medallion lights on results.

Gauntlet VIII Code Red Mission 2 — Challenge

Raid Medallion

HintThe stage compares your selected level with its recommendation.
WhereClear Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2 at or below its displayed recommended level.
Full detailBefore launching Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2, use the level-adjust command to set the character no higher than the displayed recommendation. Finish the mission and confirm the Challenge medallion on results.

Gauntlet VIII Code Red Mission 2 — No Herb

Raid Medallion

HintPlan a safe route and leave manual healing unused.
WhereClear Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2 without consuming any herb.
Full detailEnter Gauntlet VIII, Code Red, Mission 2 with a defensive build, avoid opening the herb command, and restart if an herb is consumed. Reach the goal and verify the No Herb medallion is awarded.

Gauntlet VIII Code Red Mission 2 — Genocide

Raid Gauntlet IX

You are here when: The Raid stage-select panel headed Gauntlet IX, with its difficulty tabs and mission tiles.

Every mission has five separate medallions; Completion requires the preceding four during one run.
